### [1] [An SOA-Based Big Data Management Framework for Primary Healthcare Centers in Bahrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00021)
*Hasan Abdulla,Fatema AlJazeeri,Shaikha Hasan,Zainab Almahafdha,Ehab Adwan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文基于面向服务架构（SOA）原则，为巴林某初级医疗中心设计并应用了一个名为SOA-MHC的大数据管理框架。该框架采用分层架构（表示层、服务层、数据集成层），遵循设计科学研究方法论（DSRM）的六个系统阶段进行开发。实证结果表明，SOA-MHC在数据集成与互操作性、实时分析、运营效率、成本降低及患者满意度方面均取得显著提升，有效解决了医疗大数据管理中的数据孤岛问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗行业数据快速增长带来了大数据管理的严峻挑战，特别是数据孤岛和系统互操作性不足的问题，亟需一种有效的企业架构方法来应对这些复杂性。

Method: 采用设计科学研究方法论（DSRM）的六个阶段（问题识别、目标定义、设计/开发、演示、评估、沟通），构建了SOA-MHC分层架构（表示层、服务层、数据集成层），并通过Delphi技术访谈、文档审查、观察和基准研究等多种方法收集和验证数据。

Result: SOA-MHC实施带来了显著运营效益：改善数据集成与跨系统互操作性、增强实时数据分析与临床洞察、提升运营效率、降低成本、提高患者参与度和满意度，有效缓解了碎片化数据孤岛问题。

Conclusion: SOA-MHC验证了基于SOA的企业架构在医疗大数据管理中的实际有效性，为医疗信息学中应用SOA原则提供了知识贡献，并为医疗机构的数字化转型提供了经过验证的部署路线图。

Abstract: The rapid data growth in the healthcare industry has presented significant challenges in managing big data effectively. This research designs and applies a service-oriented architecture-based approach for managing big data for a Primary Healthcare Center in Bahrain (SOA-MHC) to investigate the application of SOA principles in enterprise architecture. The research employs the Design Science Research Methodology (DSRM) to guide the development of the SOA-MHC artifact, following its six systematic phases: problem identification, objective definition, design/development, demonstration, evaluation, and communication. The SOA-MHC utilizes a layered architecture comprising a Presentation Layer, Service Layer, and Data Integration Layer. The framework leverages core SOA principles - specifically loose coupling, reusability, and interoperability - to create a modular system capable of handling the complexities of healthcare big data. Diverse data collection and validation methods were utilized, including stakeholder interviews via the Delphi technique, document reviews, observations, and benchmarking research. The study's empirical findings highlight significant operational benefits from the SOA-MHC implementation. These include improved data integration and interoperability across disparate systems, enhanced real-time data analytics and clinical insights, clear operational efficiency gains, cost reductions, and elevated patient engagement and satisfaction. The architecture demonstrates the practical efficacy of an SOA-based enterprise framework in mitigating fragmented data silos. This research contributes to the knowledge of applying SOA principles in medical informatics and provides a validated deployment roadmap for practitioners and researchers transforming data management methods in healthcare institutions.

</details>


### [2] [Stop Hand-Holding Your Coding Agent: Engineering the Loops that Replace Step-by-Step Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00038)
*Sandeco Macedo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"循环规范"(Loop Specification)概念，将其定义为一种由触发条件、目标、验证步骤、停止规则和记忆组成的有边界、可复用的制品，用于替代逐步提示(prompting)的智能体交互方式。作者区分了外部循环规范与普通编程循环及内部感知-行动-观察循环，系统分析了50个真实循环案例，提出了设计原则与反模式，并指出该实践仍面临验证负担、理解债务和认知放弃等限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2026年中期出现了一句口号——"停止提示你的智能体，开始设计提示它的循环"——这重新组织了对编码智能体的讨论方式。作者希望严肃对待这一主张，为这种新兴实践提供清晰的定义、范围界定和系统性分析，以建立"循环工程"(loop engineering)这一新学科。

Method: 1) 定义循环规范的概念和范围；2) 构建循环规范的解剖学结构(触发条件、目标类型、五级验证阶梯、架构、命名终止状态)和分类法；3) 对50个真实循环构成的公开语料库(Loop Library)进行手工编码和描述性分析；4) 基于自我纠正、奖励黑客和模型作为评判者的脆弱性等科学文献，提炼设计原则和反模式。

Result: 语料库分析显示：70%的循环在验证阶梯的自主区进行验证，74%命名了其终止状态。自动触发和持久记忆方面相对欠发达。实践在学科认为重要的方面已趋于成熟。

Conclusion: 循环工程是从提示→上下文→工具箱→循环的进阶中的新层次，但并未淘汰提示工程；循环和提示是不同工具有不同用途。实践仍需尊重其局限性，包括验证负担、理解债务和认知放弃的风险。

Abstract: In mid-2026 a slogan reorganized how practitioners talk about coding agents: stop prompting your agent, start designing the loop that prompts it. We take this claim seriously and give it a careful treatment. We call the object of the new practice the loop specification: a bounded, reusable artifact, made of a trigger, a goal, a verification step, a stopping rule and a memory, that a human hands to an agent harness (such as Claude Code or Codex) so the agent pursues a goal on its own, in place of step-by-step prompting. We distinguish this external loop specification from two things it is often confused with: an ordinary programming loop, and the internal perceive-act-observe cycle that the harness already provides as plumbing. We position loop engineering as a new layer in the progression from prompt to context to harness to loop, and we argue, against the stronger headlines, that it does not retire prompt engineering; loop and prompt are distinct tools with distinct uses. We offer four contributions: a definition and scope for the discipline; an anatomy and taxonomy of loop specifications organized around trigger, goal type, a five-level verification ladder, architecture, and named terminal states; a descriptive analysis of the Loop Library, a public corpus of fifty real loops that we code by hand; and a set of design principles and anti-patterns grounded in the scientific literature on self-correction, reward hacking and model-as-judge fragility. The corpus shows that practice has matured most where the discipline says it matters: seventy percent of loops verify in the autonomous zone of the ladder and seventy-four percent name their terminal states, while automated triggering and durable memory remain comparatively underdeveloped. We close with the limits the practice must respect, including the verification burden, comprehension debt and the risk of cognitive surrender.

</details>


### [3] [Evaluating Hardware Abstraction Layer Concepts for Software Defined Vehicles: Insights into Applicability and Effectiveness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00039)
*Akshay Narla,Johannes Stümpfle,Souvik Saha,Nasser Jazdi,Michael Weyrich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了当前汽车领域及跨领域（智能手机、网络、工业自动化）的硬件抽象层（HAL）方案，针对软件定义汽车（SDV）需求构建了标准评估框架。研究发现基于中间件的HAL在可移植性和模块化方面表现优异，而基于虚拟化Hypervisor的方案在安全性、OTA就绪性和硬件效率上更具优势。两种方案均存在局限，因此提出了融合Hypervisor隔离与中间件标准化的混合HAL设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件定义汽车（SDV）要求软硬件解耦、模块化、互操作性、实时处理和OTA更新能力，但当前汽车系统软硬件紧密耦合、复杂异构，难以满足这些需求。现有HAL在SDV场景下的适用性和有效性尚不明确，亟需系统性评估与跨领域借鉴。

Method: 1) 系统评估当前汽车领域的HAL方案；2) 探索非汽车领域（智能手机、网络、工业自动化）的HAL机制，提取跨领域洞见；3) 构建基于SDV特定需求的标准评估框架；4) 对比分析中间件方案与虚拟化Hypervisor方案在不同维度上的优劣。

Result: 中间件式HAL在可移植性和模块化方面表现更好；Hypervisor式HAL在安全性、OTA就绪性和硬件效率方面更具优势。两种方案均存在局限，单一方案无法完全满足SDV全生命周期需求。

Conclusion: 建议采用融合Hypervisor隔离能力与中间件标准化接口的混合HAL设计，以支持灵活、可扩展且面向未来的SDV架构。

Abstract: The emergence of Software-Defined Vehicles represents a fundamental shift in automotive design, prioritizing software-centric architectures over traditional hardware-driven models. SDVs require modularity, interoperability, real-time processing, and over-the-air update capabilities throughout the vehicle lifecycle. However, current vehicle systems, characterized by tightly coupled software and hardware, struggle to meet these demands due to their complexity and heterogeneity. A critical first step toward enabling SDVs is the decoupling of software from hardware, which can be facilitated through a robust Hardware Abstraction Layer. While existing HALs offer hardware independence and standardized interfaces, their applicability and effectiveness in SDV contexts remain uncertain. This paper systematically evaluates current automotive HALs and explores HAL mechanisms from non-automotive domains, including smartphones, networking, and industrial automation, to extract cross-domain insights relevant to SDV development. A criteria-driven evaluation framework is developed to assess HALs against SDV-specific needs. Findings reveal that while middleware-based HALs offer portability and modularity, hypervisor-based approaches better support safety, OTA readiness, and hardware efficiency. Limitations in both approaches are identified, prompting recommendations for a hybrid HAL design that integrates hypervisor isolation with middleware standardization. This paper contributes to the ongoing developments in automotive software architecture by offering insights into the applicability and effectiveness of current HAL strategies. It provides actionable guidance for designing flexible, scalable, and future-ready HALs to support SDVs across their lifecycle.

</details>


### [4] [ATM: CID-Brokered Pre-Write Admission for Multi-Agent Code Co-Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00041)
*Eagl Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ATM框架通过规范驱动的治理链（绑定意图、范围、准入、验证和证据）解决多智能体LLM系统中的并发写意图协调问题，使用CID代理和原子化映射确保共享变更有序、可审计和可恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统在分解软件工程任务时面临一个关键的底层系统问题：对于并发形成的写意图，系统必须决定哪些可以并行执行、哪些需要确定性组合或串行化、哪些必须走失败关闭路径，现有方法缺乏规范驱动的治理机制。

Method: ATM框架通过以下机制实现规范驱动的治理：1) CID代理作为共享突变准入子系统；2) 适配器引导的原子化映射将写意图映射到语义原子和有界区域，原子映射覆盖不完整时使用虚拟原子作为临时可审计治理单元；3) 受控共享写入最终由中立管理员而非提议代理直接应用。

Result: 通过12场景确定性设计矩阵、三个存档runner案例、ATM-AdmissionBench、三个存档同文件边界案例、三周外部采用者研究和操作恢复路由基准等多维度评估，证明了框架在单域设置下的可行性、可审计性和有界可恢复性。

Conclusion: ATM框架在单治理域内有效解决了多智能体并发写意图的协调问题，但不主张广泛的比较优势或跨克隆治理能力。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems can decompose software-engineering work into planning, generation, validation, and repair, but a narrower systems problem remains: before any governed shared mutation is applied, a system must decide which concurrently formed write intents may proceed in parallel, which require deterministic composition or serialization, and which must take a fail-closed path. We address this problem with the AI-Atomic-Framework (ATM), a specification-grounded governance substrate for software agents operating within a single governance domain.
  ATM binds task intent, repository scope, write admission, validation, and evidence obligations into one governance chain. A Content Identifier (CID) broker serves as the shared-mutation admission subsystem. Adapter-guided atomization maps write intents to semantic atoms and bounded regions; when persistent atom-map coverage is incomplete, virtual atoms provide temporary auditable governance units for conservative comparison and routing. Governed shared writes are ultimately applied by a neutral steward rather than directly by proposing agents.
  Evaluation combines controlled, field, adoption, and extension evidence, including a 12-scenario deterministic design matrix, three archived runner cases, ATM-AdmissionBench, three archived same-file boundary cases, a three-week external-adopter study, and an operational recovery-routing benchmark. The results support feasibility, auditability, and bounded recoverability within the observed single-domain settings, but do not claim broad comparative superiority or cross-clone governance.

</details>


### [5] [A Taxonomy of Single-Turn Textual Prompt Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00043)
*Vennila Sooben,Eugene Syriani*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对单轮文本交互场景，通过可重复方法识别出30个独特的规范提示模式，并沿两个维度构建了提示模式的分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中"提示模式"的定义不统一，分类方式各异，缺乏系统化的单轮文本交互提示模式分类体系。

Method: 采用可重复的研究方法，对单轮、基于文本的交互场景进行系统分析，识别并整理出30个独特的规范提示模式，并沿两个维度进行组织分类。

Result: 识别出30个独特且规范的提示模式，构建了沿两个维度的提示模式分类体系（taxonomy）。

Conclusion: 本文为单轮文本交互中的提示模式提供了系统化的分类框架，有助于统一提示工程领域的术语和分类标准。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are now widely employed in software development and everyday use. Interacting with LLMs requires crafting prompts, which range from simple ad hoc sentences to extensive, detailed, and structured instructions. Knowledge about prompt engineering has been documented in several surveys and catalogs in the literature. However, the term ``prompt pattern'' is defined differently across sources, and existing works have classified prompt patterns in different ways. In this report, we present a taxonomy of prompt patterns for single-turn, text-based interactions. Following a reproducible method, we identified 30 unique and canonical prompt patterns, organized along two dimensions.

</details>


### [6] [Comparing Large Language Models on Scrum Certification-Style Questions: Accuracy, Stability, and Error Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00048)
*Robson Alves Vilar,Emanuel Dantas Filho,Ademar França de Sousa Neto,Mirko Perkusich,Danyllo Wagner Albuquerque,João Paiva,Kyller Gorgônio,Angelo Perkusich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了GPT-5 mini、Gemini 3 Flash和DeepSeek Chat 3.2三个LLM在993道Scrum认证题上的表现，采用零样本、链式思考和源引用三种提示策略。结果显示Gemini 3 Flash准确率最高，模型在单一选择题上表现最佳，在多选题和判断题上错误率较高；错误模式呈系统性，包括过度泛化、限制性措辞、复合干扰项及市场解释与Scrum定义冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在软件工程领域的认证考试问答场景中日益普及，但系统评估其对规范性定义、角色、工件和规则遵循能力的研究尚不充分。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过标准化的Scrum认证评估框架，量化评估不同LLM在严格遵循领域规范性知识方面的表现差异。

Method: 选取993道符合PSM I评估格式的Scrum认证风格题目，使用三个LLM（GPT-5 mini、Gemini 3 Flash、DeepSeek Chat 3.2）在零样本、链式思考和源引用三种提示策略下进行测试，通过重复执行评估模型内稳定性，并按Scrum主题和问题格式分析性能，辅以错误模式的定性分析。

Result: Gemini 3 Flash准确率最高，GPT-5 mini次之，DeepSeek Chat 3.2最低；模型内变异性在不同条件下均较低。单一选择题准确率最高，多选和判断题错误率较高。在Artifacts、Empiricism、Product Value等规范性明确的主题上表现一致，在Scrum Values、Self-Managing Teams、Stakeholders & Customers等主题上表现较弱。错误呈现系统性特征。

Conclusion: 不同LLM在严格遵循Scrum规范性知识方面存在显著性能差异；模型错误呈现系统性而非随机性，包括过度泛化、限制性措辞、复合干扰项以及市场常见解释与严格Scrum定义之间的冲突，表明LLM在需要严格遵循领域规范的知识密集型任务中仍存在根本性局限。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in exam- and certification-style question answering tasks, where their ability to retrieve, interpret, and apply domain-specific knowledge can be systematically assessed. In Software Engineering, such settings are particularly relevant when questions depend on strict adherence to normative definitions, roles, artifacts, and rules. This paper evaluates the performance of three contemporary LLMs, \textit{GPT-5 mini}, \textit{Gemini 3 Flash}, and \textit{DeepSeek Chat 3.2}, in answering 993 Scrum certification-style questions aligned with the Professional Scrum Master I (PSM I) assessment format. We evaluated the models under three prompting strategies (\textit{zero-shot}, \textit{chain-of-thought}, and \textit{source-grounded}), with repeated executions to assess intra-model stability. We also analyzed performance across Scrum topics and question formats, complemented by a qualitative analysis of recurring error patterns in incorrect answers. Results revealed clear differences among models, with Gemini 3 Flash achieving the highest accuracy, followed by GPT-5 mini and DeepSeek Chat 3.2, while intra-model variability remained low across all conditions. By question format, the models achieved the highest accuracy on single-answer multiple-choice items, whereas multi-select and True/False questions were more error-prone. By topic, performance was more consistent in normatively explicit areas such as Artifacts, Empiricism, and Product Value, but more fragile in Scrum Values, Self-Managing Teams, and Stakeholders \& Customers. The qualitative analysis showed that errors were systematic rather than random, involving overgeneralization, restrictive wording, compound distractors, and conflicts between common market interpretations and strict Scrum definitions.

</details>


### [7] [Prompting GPT-5 on Scrum Certification Questions: An Empirical Accuracy Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00049)
*Mirko Perkusich,Danyllo Albuquerque,João Paiva,Robson Vilar,Emanuel Dantas,Ademar França de Sousa Neto,Rohit Gheyi,Kyller Gorgônio,Angelo Perkusich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了不同提示技术（零样本、思维链、带来源引用）对GPT-5回答Scrum认证问题的准确性影响。基于993道PSM认证题目的测试显示，所有提示方法的准确率均超过85%，其中带来源引用的变体表现最佳（89.1%）。错误主要集中在多选问题和解释性领域（如Scrum团队和产品价值），错误类型包括与Scrum指南不一致（28%）、超出其范围（34%）以及过时或有偏见的解释（38%）。提示技术能适度提升可靠性，尤其在减少误解和版本漂移方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM越来越多地被用于敏捷软件开发的文档、辅导和培训，特别是在准备Professional Scrum Master（PSM）等认证考试时，需要评估LLM是否能够可靠地推理Scrum这一具有规范性、明确定义规则的框架。核心动机是验证LLM在Scrum这一规则明确的领域中的事实准确性，以及提示技术能否提升其表现。

Method: 使用993道经过验证的PSM认证风格问题，通过GPT-5模型采用三种提示技术（零样本、思维链、带来源引用）进行回答，然后评估其准确性和错误模式。

Result: 所有提示技术均达到超过85%的认证级准确率，带来源引用的变体表现最佳（89.1%），错误率最低。正确回答集中在定义完成、事件和产品待办列表管理等明确定义的主题以及单项选择题上。多选问题和更具解释性的领域（Scrum团队和产品价值）表现不稳定。16.2%的题目至少有一个提示变体失败，错误类型分布为：与Scrum指南不一致（28%）、超出范围（34%）、过时或有偏见的解释（38%）。

Conclusion: 提示技术能带来适度但一致的改进，特别是在减少误解和版本漂移方面，支持LLM在敏捷学习和认证准备中的更可靠使用。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in Agile Software Development for documentation, coaching, and training. As practitioners adopt these tools to prepare for certifications such as Professional Scrum Master (PSM), a key question is whether LLMs can reliably reason about Scrum, a framework with normative, well-defined rules described in the Scrum Guide (2020). This paper examines how different prompt techniques affect the factual accuracy of LLM responses to Scrum certification-style questions. A dataset of 993 validated PSM-aligned questions was answered by GPT-5 using three techniques: zero-shot, chain-of-thought, and with-source citation. All prompts achieved certification-level accuracy above 85\%, with the citation-based variant performing best (89.1\%) and yielding the lowest error rate. Correct answers concentrated in well-defined topics, such as \emph{Definition of Done}, Events, and Product Backlog Management, and in single-answer multiple-choice items, while multi-select questions and more interpretive areas, such as Scrum Team and Product Value, were less stable. Among questions where at least one prompt failed (16.2\%), errors clustered into misalignment with the Scrum Guide (28\%), content outside its scope (34\%), and outdated or biased interpretations (38\%). Overall, prompt techniques produced modest but consistent improvements, particularly in reducing misinterpretation and version drift, supporting more reliable use of LLMs in Agile learning and certification preparation.

</details>


### [8] [SWE-Router: Routing in Multi-turn Agentic Software Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00053)
*Seongho Son,Sangwoong Yoon,Jiahua Tang,Shuhan Wang,Lorenz Wolf,Ilija Bogunovic*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出SWE-Router，一种基于价值的时间路由方法，先让廉价模型执行若干探索轮次，根据局部轨迹决定是否升级到昂贵模型，从而在软件工程任务中实现成本效益优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM路由器仅基于任务描述进行路由，在智能体场景中存在信息论贝叶斯误差下限——类似的问题描述可能对应局部笔误或多模块重构，提示无法区分二者，导致路由决策不准确。

Method: SWE-Router采用基于价值的时间路由方法：先让廉价模型运行若干探索轮次，基于部分轨迹判断是否继续使用廉价模型还是升级到昂贵模型，并提供了贝叶斯最优性理论证明。

Result: 在跨当代成本-能力边界的弱-强LLM配对实验中，SWE-Router显著提升了软件工程任务的成本效率，同时保留了强模型的大部分性能。

Conclusion: 条件化于部分轨迹的路由策略永远不会损害路由效果，且当探索提供信息时严格更优；该方法可有效降低SWE任务成本。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) embedded in multi-turn agentic harnesses are reshaping software engineering (SWE), but routing every task to a frontier model is wasteful when many issues admit cheap fixes. Existing LLM routers operate on the task description alone, which inherits an information-theoretic Bayes-error floor in agentic settings: a similar issue can hide either a localized typo or a multi-module refactor, and the prompt does not separate the two. We introduce SWE-Router, a value-based temporal approach that lets a cheap model run for a few exploratory turns and reads the resulting partial trajectory before deciding whether to continue cheaply or to escalate to an expensive model. We provide a Bayes-optimality theorem showing that conditioning on the partial trajectory never harms routing and is strictly better whenever exploration is informative. Across the LLM pairs of weak and strong models spanning the contemporary cost--capability frontier, we show that SWE-Router greatly improves the cost efficiency of SWE tasks, while maintaining the majority of the performances of the stronger model. We additionally release a multi-LLM trajectory dataset which allows reproduction of our trajectory-level routing.

</details>


### [9] [A Quantitative Framework for Estimating System Complexity and Cost via Component Interface Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00054)
*Ken Y. Chan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个形式化建模框架，通过有向耦合图表示系统依赖关系，利用组件接口估计变更复杂度，并结合外部驱动因素与成本因子量化系统级修改成本。该框架支持图形、代数和表格多视图表示，并通过大型零售银行平台集成案例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以准确估计外部需求引发的系统变更复杂度与成本，尤其是在组件内部逻辑不透明的情况下，缺乏形式化的建模手段来桥接组件建模与项目成本估算之间的鸿沟。

Method: 将系统建模为有向耦合图，通过组件接口估计有界变更复杂度；将外部驱动因素与成本因子关联到系统元素，实现系统级修改成本的有界量化；提供图形、代数和表格三种视图表示。

Result: 通过大型零售银行平台集成案例验证了框架的有效性，表明该方法能够为架构设计、软件工程和生命周期运维提供可操作的见解。

Conclusion: 提出的框架有效桥接了组件建模与项目成本估算，为应对外部需求驱动的系统变更提供了形式化的复杂度与成本估算手段。

Abstract: This paper introduces a formal modeling framework designed to estimate the complexity and cost associated with system changes induced by external requirements. We model a system as a directed graph of couplings, capturing the intricate dependencies and information flows between components and elements within a specific context. The proposed method enables the estimation of bounded change complexity through component interfaces, even when internal logic remains opaque. Additionally, the framework provides a mechanism for bounding the cost of system-wide modifications by associating external drivers and cost factors with individual system elements. We propose a multi-view approach to the model, providing graphical, algebraic, and tabular representations to suit different levels of abstraction and computational needs. By bridging the gap between component-based modeling and project cost estimation, our method provides actionable insights for architecture design, software engineering, and lifecycle operations. The model is validated through a case study involving the integration of a large-scale retail banking platform.

</details>


### [10] [AlgoBench: Benchmarking Algorithmic Adaptation in Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00062)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ALGOBENCH是一个通过结构化约束变换自动构建新颖算法问题的框架，用于评估LLM的算法推理能力而非仅功能性正确性。实验表明，模型在ALGOBENCH变体上性能显著下降，许多正确解无法达到所需复杂度，错误主要是算法层面而非实现层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编程基准（如HumanEval、LiveCodeBench）的高通过率并不能真实反映模型的算法推理能力，因为固定基准可能通过问题陈述、题解和生成解决方案泄露到公共训练生态中，使后续模型因暴露而非更强的算法能力而改进。

Method: 提出ALGOBENCH框架，通过结构化约束变换（constraint-shifting transformations）从已知竞赛编程问题自动构建新颖算法问题变体。每个变体可追溯至源问题，但必须使原始参考算法失效。引入复杂度感知指标（OPTT、OPTS、TRAPRATE、GAPT、CONSENS）评估解决方案的功能正确性和渐近复杂度适宜性。

Result: 多LLM和提示策略实验显示：模型在ALGOBENCH变体上性能急剧下降；检索（retrieval）会增加对旧算法的重用；许多看似正确的解决方案未能达到所需复杂度要求；错误主要属于算法层面而非实现层面。

Conclusion: ALGOBENCH能够评估模型超越功能性正确性的算法适应能力（adaptation），现有LLM在算法推理和复杂度适配方面仍存在显著不足。

Abstract: High pass rates on established programming benchmarks such as HumanEval and LiveCodeBench do not always show whether a model can reason about algorithms. Many fixed benchmarks eventually become part of the public training ecosystem through released problem statements, editorials, and generated solutions, allowing later models to improve partly by exposure rather than by stronger algorithmic ability. We introduce ALGOBENCH, a framework that automatically builds novel algorithmic problems from known competitive-programming problems through structured constraint-shifting transformations. Each accepted ALGOBENCH variant is traceable to a source problem, but must make the original reference algorithm fail. Beyond pass@$k$, we introduce complexity-aware metrics -- including OPTT, OPTS, TRAPRATE, GAPT, and CONSENS -- to test whether a solution is not only functionally correct but also asymptotically suitable for the generated problem. Experiments across multiple LLMs and prompting strategies show that performance drops sharply on ALGOBENCH variants, retrieval can increase reuse of the old algorithm, and many correct-looking solutions fail to meet the required complexity. Error analysis shows that failures are mainly algorithmic rather than implementation-level, suggesting that ALGOBENCH evaluates adaptation beyond functional correctness.

</details>


### [11] [The Illusion of Safety: Multi-Tier Verification of AI vs. Human C++ Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00107)
*Saif Mahmud,Fadul Sikder,Yuede Ji,Zhang Haotian,Jeff,Lei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文构建VULBENCH-CPP基准（8,918个C++程序），通过功能测试、静态分析、动态分析和有界模型检测四层验证，发现AI生成代码触发运行时漏洞的概率约为人类代码的两倍，且静态分析因代码长度混淆而低估了这一差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代码安全评估多依赖静态分析，但静态分析仅报告警告、无法确认运行时违规，且安全社区对AI生成C++代码是否比人类代码更不安全缺乏定量证据。

Method: 构建VULBENCH-CPP基准，包含来自3个开源LLM（Gemma 3 27B IT、LLaMA 3.3 70B Instruct、Qwen 2.5 Coder 32B Instruct）和人类作者在851个竞赛任务上生成的8,918个C++程序，通过四层验证（功能测试、静态分析cppcheck/clang-tidy、动态分析ASan/UBSan、有界模型检测ESBMC）进行标注，并控制代码长度和测试通过率等混杂因素。

Result: AI生成代码触发确认运行时违规的概率约为人类代码的两倍；静态分析下两者表面安全水平相似，但这种相似性由代码长度混淆造成而非真实安全水平；不同验证层级检测到的违规类型差异大，单一层级不足以评估安全风险；该差距在多次独立生成中一致保持。

Conclusion: AI生成的C++代码客观上比人类代码更不安全，静态分析无法揭示这一差距，需要多层级验证工具联合评估才能准确衡量AI代码的安全风险。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate C++, a memory-unsafe language where a single overlooked violation can become an exploitable bug. Yet most security evaluations of AI-generated code rely on static analysis alone, which flags warnings without confirming runtime violations or reasoning about untested paths. We ask whether AI-generated C++ is measurably less safe than human-written code, and whether common verification tools agree on the risk. We introduce VULBENCH-CPP, a benchmark of 8,918 C++ programs from three open-weight LLMs (Gemma 3 27B IT, LLaMA 3.3 70B Instruct, Qwen 2.5 Coder 32B Instruct) and human authors across 851 competitive-programming tasks. Each program is annotated by four verification tiers: functional testing, static analysis (cppcheck, clang-tidy), dynamic analysis (ASan/UBSan), and bounded model checking (ESBMC). Accounting for the correlation among solutions to a shared task, we find that AI-generated code is roughly twice as likely as human code to trigger a confirmed runtime violation, even after controlling for code length and test pass-rate. Under static analysis the two look equally safe, but this is misleading: the apparent similarity reflects code length rather than real safety, and the tiers detect largely different classes of violation, so no single tier is sufficient. The gap is consistent across independent generations.

</details>


### [12] [Registry-Governed Agent Lifecycle:Completing EDDOps with Evaluation-DrivenRegistration, Promotion, and Retirement on AWS AgentCore](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00345)
*Richard Kang,Vincent Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EDDOps框架，将评估作为LLM Agent全生命周期的持续治理功能，并在AWS Bedrock AgentCore上实现，通过成本-性能框架指导企业模型选择，将模型选择从基准排名转变为受治理的经济决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业采用LLM Agent需要兼顾质量、可靠性、安全性、延迟和成本的模型选择方法，而传统TDD/BDD方法不适用于非确定性LLM Agent，因此需要将评估作为持续治理功能的新范式。

Method: 提出了EDDOps参考架构在AWS Bedrock AgentCore上的实例化，包括Runtime、Evaluations、Agent Registry和CloudWatch可观测性组件；构建了成本-性能决策框架；通过30次单轮调用、9次多轮评估和注册表集成治理的PoC验证三个基础模型的两种部署路径。

Result: 评估证据可以将模型选择从基准排名练习转变为受治理的经济决策；托管Agent平台若提供轨迹原生可观测性、可插拔评估框架和受治理的注册表发现，即可支持EDDOps。

Conclusion: EDDOps为企业在实际生产环境中选择LLM模型提供了系统化的评估驱动方法，弥补了传统测试方法在非确定性AI系统中的不足，实现了质量与经济性的平衡决策。

Abstract: Enterprise adoption of LLM agents requires model selection methods that balance quality, reliability, safety, latency, and cost. Evaluation-Driven Development and Operations (EDDOps) positions evaluation as a continuous governing function across the agent lifecycle rather than a terminal checkpoint. This paper presents a practitioner-oriented instantiation of EDDOps on AWS Bedrock AgentCore and proposes a cost-to-performance framework for selecting foundation models in enterprise agent architectures. We make three contributions: a conceptual synthesis explaining why traditional TDD/BDD methods are insufficient for non-deterministic LLM agents; an architectural mapping of the EDDOps reference architecture onto AgentCore Runtime, Evaluations, Agent Registry, and CloudWatch observability; and an empirical cost-to-performance decision framework validated through a proof-of-concept comparing three foundation models across two deployment paths. Using trace data from 30 single-turn invocations across six agents, 9 multi-turn evaluations, and registry-integrated governance, we show how evaluation evidence can convert model selection from a benchmark-ranking exercise into a governed economic decision. The results suggest that managed agent platforms can support EDDOps when they provide trace-native observability, pluggable evaluator frameworks, and governed registry-based discovery.

</details>


### [13] [BT-APE: A Computationally Light Backtracking Approach to Automatic Prompt Engineering for Requirements Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00427)
*Mohammad Amin Zadenoori,Waad Alhoshan,Jacek Dąbrowski,Liping Zhao,Alessio Ferrari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级自动提示工程方法BT-APE，用于需求工程中的分类任务，在保持与资源密集型方法相当精度的同时，显著降低了计算开销（减少72%输入token和66%运行时间）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在需求工程任务中日益普及，但提示设计通常依赖手动试错，效果不一致且次优；自动提示构建在RE领域尚未被充分探索，其有效性不明。

Method: 将提示设计建模为优化问题，通过LLM生成候选提示、回溯搜索和动态示例选择进行迭代优化，提出轻量级Backtracking APE（BT-APE）方法。

Result: 在三个基准数据集、五个指令微调LLM上，BT-APE与资源密集型APE基线（PE2）精度几乎相同，均显著优于经典提示方法（零样本、少样本、思维链等），但BT-APE消耗约72%更少输入token和66%更少运行时间。

Conclusion: BT-APE是一种高效、轻量的自动提示工程方案，适合资源受限的部署场景；贡献包括：轻量APE框架及开源工具、首次系统对比APE与经典提示在需求分类上的效果、揭示了类别定义和提示演化对性能的影响。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to requirements engineering (RE) tasks, yet the prompts guiding them are typically designed manually through trial and error, yielding inconsistent and suboptimal results. Automated prompt construction remains largely unexplored in RE, leaving its effectiveness unclear. To address this, we propose a lightweight Automatic Prompt Engineering approach, Backtracking APE (BT-APE), and apply it to requirements classification. We frame prompt design as an optimization problem, iteratively refining prompts via LLM-generated candidates, backtracking search, and dynamic example selection. Evaluating BT-APE on three benchmark datasets with five instruction-tuned LLMs, we compare it against four classical prompting baselines (zero-shot, few-shot, chain-of-thought, CoT+few-shot) and a state-of-the-art but resource-intensive APE baseline (PE2). BT-APE and PE2 achieve nearly identical accuracy, both substantially outperforming the classical baselines with large effect sizes; however, BT-APE imposes a far lighter computational footprint, consuming roughly 72% fewer input tokens and 66% less wall-clock time at equivalent accuracy, making it better suited to resource-constrained deployment. Our contributions are threefold: (i) a lightweight APE framework with an open interactive tool and replication package; (ii) the first systematic comparison of APE against classical prompting for requirements classification; and (iii) insights into how class definitions and prompt evolution affect performance.

</details>


### [14] [Social Popularity of GitHub Projects: A Lifeline or a Liability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00435)
*Mohit Kaushik,Kuljit Kaur Chahal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对GitHub上超过73,000个仓库的生存分析，发现人力资本是项目生存最关键的决定因素，而过度的社会关注反而成为负担，当与代码可读性和文档等易用性特征结合时，会加速项目停止活跃开发的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交编码平台（如GitHub）上大量仓库面临高死亡率问题，但影响项目生存的多个因素（尤其是社会关注的双刃剑效应以及结构性特征与可见性的交互作用）尚未被充分理解。

Method: 使用加速失效时间（AFT）生存分析框架，分析超过73,000个GitHub仓库，该框架能够处理预测变量随时间变化的特性。

Result: 人力资本是项目生存的最关键因素；过度社会关注是负担；当社会关注与易用性特征（代码可读性、文档）结合时，项目停止活跃开发的风险被放大；贡献者数量与社会流行度的交互作用揭示了劳动力的保护效应。

Conclusion: 需要制定平衡可见性与劳动力容量的治理策略，以确保开源项目的长期韧性。

Abstract: Social coding platforms such as GitHub host millions of repositories, yet many suffer from high mortality rates. Despite this, several survival factors remain poorly understood. Human capital is widely recognized as essential. Social attention, while often assumed to be a lifeline, can become a liability. Structural features that improve onboarding, such as code readability and documentation, may also accelerate the cessation of active development when combined with massive visibility. To examine these dynamics, we analyzed more than 73,000 GitHub repositories using an Accelerated Failure Time (AFT) survival framework, which accounts for the time-varying nature of predictors. Our study identifies human capital as the most critical determinant of project survival. In contrast, excessive social attention emerges as a liability, and when coupled with accessibility features, it amplifies the risk of project inactivity. Importantly, when the number of contributors interacts with social popularity, the protective effect of labor becomes visible, highlighting the need for governance strategies that balance visibility with labor capacity to ensure the long-term resilience of open-source projects.

</details>


### [15] [Large Language Models for Multi-Lingual Equivalent Mutant Detection: An Extended Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00511)
*Honglin Shu,Zhao Tian,Dong Wang,Junji Yu,Jiazhe Zhang,Xuejie Cao,Junjie Chen,Yasutaka Kamei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了大型语言模型（LLM）在等价变异体检测（EMD）中的应用，基于3,302个Java和1,088个C变异体对进行实验，结果表明基于LLM的方法在F1分数上优于传统方法，微调后的代码嵌入策略检测精度最高，且微调后的LLM展现出跨语言泛化能力，在有效性和效率之间取得了良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等价变异体（equivalent mutants）的存在增加了变异测试的不必要成本和偏差，降低了其实用性。现有等价变异体检测方法面临挑战：纯代码分析方法受限于特定编译器基础设施，而机器学习方法面临训练数据稀缺和泛化能力有限的问题。LLM在代码相关任务中展现了强大能力，但其在EMD领域的潜力尚未被充分探索，特别是在多语言环境下。

Method: 本文构建了包含3,302个Java变异体对和1,088个C变异体对的基准数据集，系统评估了LLM在EMD任务中的表现。实验对比了多种策略：直接使用LLM进行判别、基于代码嵌入的微调方法等，并与现有最先进的传统EMD方法进行对比。评估维度包括检测准确性（F1分数）、策略变体效果、推理效率和跨语言泛化能力。

Result: 基于LLM的方法在F1分数上优于评估的传统方法；微调后的代码嵌入（fine-tuned code embedding）在所有测试策略中取得了最高的检测精度；LLM方法的推理时间与现有机器学习模型相当，在有效性和效率之间实现了实际平衡；微调后的LLM展现出可测量的跨编程语言泛化能力。

Conclusion: LLM为解决等价变异体检测这一长期挑战提供了一种可行且高效的方法，为推进变异测试的实践应用开辟了新的方向。

Abstract: Mutation testing is a powerful technique for ensuring software quality. However, the presence of equivalent mutants introduces unnecessary costs and biases, limiting its practical effectiveness. Although numerous equivalent mutant detection (EMD) methods have been proposed, they often face distinct challenges: pure-code analysis methods can be limited by their reliance on specific compiler infrastructures, while existing machine-learning approaches remain constrained by scarce training data and limited generalization to unseen mutants. Large language models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated remarkable performance across diverse code-related tasks by better capturing program semantics. Yet their potential for EMD remains largely unexplored, particularly in the multi-lingual context. This paper presents the first comprehensive empirical study on LLMs for EMD, using 3,302 Java and 1,088 C mutant pairs to benchmark against state-of-the-art methods, explore strategy variations, assess efficiency, and evaluate cross-lingual generalization. Experimental results show that LLM-based approaches achieve higher F1-scores than the evaluated traditional methods, with fine-tuned code embedding yielding the highest detection accuracy among the tested strategies. Moreover, LLM-based approaches strike a practical balance between effectiveness and efficiency with inference times comparable to existing machine-learning models. Importantly, fine-tuned LLMs demonstrate measurable generalization across programming languages. These findings establish LLMs as a viable and efficient approach for tackling the longstanding challenge of equivalent mutant detection, offering new directions for advancing mutation testing in practice.

</details>


### [16] [Auditing Empirical Comparisons in Quantum Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00516)
*Boshuai Ye,Peng Liang,Maryam Tavassoli Sabzevari,Arif Ali Khan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出CLAIMSTAB-QC框架，用于审计量子软件论文中经验比较的可靠性。对119篇论文中的455个比较声明进行审计，发现仅有8个声明能直接审计，结果中2个支持、4个未决、2个反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件论文中经常报告某个编译器、优化器或后端的性能优于另一个，但这些比较结果会随基准范围、电路构造、编译方式、采样方法、噪声假设等因素变化。现有测试和可重复性方法无法直接审计这些比较声明是否被证据充分支持。

Method: 提出CLAIMSTAB-QC源约束框架：记录基线、度量、关系和可接受证据；在计算结果前锁定比较设计；输出有范围的关联结果或明确的证据边界。对严格标量方向性比较，将报告方向分类为Sustained（维持）、Unresolved（未决）或Reversed（反转）。

Result: 在119篇论文的455个比较声明中，175个可表示为审计计划，79个成为标量方向性计划记录，53个可锁定审计或诊断设计，仅8个有足够匹配证据进行原始比较审计。8个记录得到2个Sustained、4个Unresolved、2个Reversed结果。对24个基准相关比较的诊断显示，简单检查可能保留表面上支持但实际证据不足的方向。

Conclusion: 量子软件论文中存在显著的材料化差距（materialization gap），大量比较声明缺乏足够的证据支撑。CLAIMSTAB-QC框架能够系统性地揭示这一差距，并为未来量子软件研究的可审计性和可重复性提供方法论基础。

Abstract: Empirical quantum-software papers often report that one compiler, optimizer, backend, or ansatz outperforms another. Such comparisons are not properties of a tool alone: they can change with benchmark scope, circuit construction, compilation, sampling, backend or noise assumptions, optimizer choices, and resource budgets. Existing testing, benchmarking, and reproducibility methods help assess programs, tools, executions, and platforms, but they do not directly audit whether the reported comparison itself is supported by the evidence exposed in the source paper or accompanying materials.
  We present CLAIMSTAB-QC, a source-bounded framework for auditing empirical comparisons in quantum software. Given a reported comparison, the framework records the baselines, metric, relation, and admissible evidence; locks the comparison design before outcomes are computed; and reports either a scoped relation outcome or an explicit evidence boundary. For strict scalar-directional comparisons, the reported direction is classified as Sustained, Unresolved, or Reversed within the locked audit scope.
  We evaluate CLAIMSTAB-QC on 455 comparative claims from 119 quantum-software papers. The central finding is a materialization gap: 175 claims can be represented for audit planning, 79 become scalar-directional planning records, 53 yield lockable audit or diagnostic designs, and only 8 expose enough matched evidence to audit the original comparison without proxy reconstruction. These 8 records yield 2 Sustained, 4 Unresolved, and 2 Reversed outcomes. Controlled diagnostics over 24 benchmark-relevant comparisons further show that simpler checks can preserve apparent directions whose support weakens under locked audit designs.

</details>


### [17] [Rise From The Ashes: LLM-based Static Analysis for Deep Learning Framework Bugs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00555)
*Shaoyu Yang,Haifeng Lin,Chunrong Fang,Xiang Chen,Wei Cheng,Jiawei Liu,Yiyu Zhang,Hongyu Liu,Zhenyu Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出Phoenix，首个基于LLM的深度学习框架静态分析技术，通过多智能体工作流建模跨语言张量流并利用结构化语义桥接中间表示检测bug，已在PyTorch中发现31个新bug。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习框架是关键的AI基础设施，但常隐藏具有严重安全影响的bug。动态方法(如fuzzing)需实际执行且成本高，静态分析虽可互补，但由于框架的多语言架构和张量相关程序状态，现有工作有限。

Method: 提出SBIR(结构化语义桥接中间表示)建模跨语言张量流；设计多智能体工作流：1) 摘要智能体从历史补丁和CWE规则提取bug摘要；2) 提取智能体根据摘要识别bug相关仓库符号；3) 生成智能体从检索上下文合成SBIR；4) 分析智能体检查SBIR并报告潜在bug。

Result: 在PyTorch针对不同异构硬件后端(Intel CPU、NVIDIA CUDA、Apple MPS)发现31个真实新bug，其中20个已合并到上游。

Conclusion: Phoenix是动态DL框架测试的有效补充，证明了LLM在DL框架静态分析中的可行性。

Abstract: Deep learning (DL) frameworks are critical AI infrastructures that often hide bugs with serious security implications. While dynamic approaches such as fuzzing are effective in uncovering these bugs, they require real test execution and incur high computational costs. Static analysis is a natural complement because it can detect bugs without runtime execution, offering fast and scalable testing. Unfortunately, there is still limited work targeting static analysis for DL frameworks due to their multilingual architectures and tensor-related program state.
  We present Phoenix, the first LLM-based static analysis technique for DL frameworks. Our key insight is that cross-language tensor flows in DL frameworks can be modeled, together with concrete code context, as a structured semantic bridge intermediate representation (SBIR) that LLMs can analyze for potential bugs in tensor semantic propagation. We implement this insight through a multi-agent workflow. A summarization agent first distills bug summaries from historical bug-fix patches and CWE rules. Guided by each summary, an extraction agent identifies bug-relevant repository symbols for code retrieval, and a generation agent synthesizes grounded SBIRs from the retrieved context. Finally, an analysis agent is leveraged to check SBIRs and report potential bugs. Our evaluation shows that Phoenix is a practical complement to dynamic DL framework testing for bug finding. To date, Phoenix has found 31 real new bugs in PyTorch for different heterogeneous hardware backends (Intel CPU, NVIDIA CUDA, and Apple MPS). Among them, 20 submitted bug-fixing patches have been merged into upstream.

</details>


### [18] [A Methodology for Investigating AI Patterns Prevalence in Software Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00558)
*Srinath Perera,Hasinthaka Piyumal,Frank Leymann,Rania Khalaf*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合文献挖掘和主动学习的方法论，用于识别AI模式并验证其在真实代码库中的普遍性。通过挖掘44篇文献识别出14类AI模式，并利用主动学习在100个GitHub开源AI仓库中评估最常见模式类的出现频率，模型在8分类任务中准确率56%、召回率55%，显著优于随机基线，为AI模式的实际应用提供了经验数据基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量AI模式被提出，但它们在真实代码中的实际使用情况尚未得到验证。理解这些模式在实际中的应用对于明确其重要性和实用性至关重要，而目前该领域缺乏经验数据支撑。

Method: 首先通过挖掘44篇已发表的AI模式相关文献，识别出14类AI模式；然后采用主动学习方法，在100个GitHub开源AI仓库中评估最常见模式类的出现频率；并使用普遍性估计方法给出模式应用准确性的置信边界。

Result: 模型在8分类任务中达到56%的准确率和55%的召回率，显著优于11%的随机基线；普遍性估计为分析模式应用提供了可用的置信边界。

Conclusion: 该方法论为理解AI模式在实际中的使用提供了稳健基础，填补了该领域缺乏经验数据的空白。

Abstract: As Artificial Intelligence(AI)-based applications take off, a clear understanding of AI patterns can uplift the quality of AI applications. Many AI patterns have been proposed in the literature; however, their prevalence in real-life code has not yet been validated. Understanding the actual use of those patterns in practice can clarify our understanding both of the significance of these patterns and their utility. In this paper, we present a methodology to a) identify relevant patterns by mining the literature and then to b) validate their presence and prevalence in actual code repositories using active learning. To that end, we identify 14 AI pattern classes by mining 44 published AI pattern-related sources. Then we use an active learning approach to determine the prevalence of the most common pattern class across 100 GitHub open AI repositories. Using prevalence estimation, we propose bounds on the accuracy of the occurrences. The model achieves 56\% accuracy and 55\% recall in an 8-way classification task, significantly outperforming the 11\% random-chance baseline. Furthermore, the prevalence estimation offers usable bounds for analyzing pattern applications. This methodology provides a robust foundation to start understanding how AI patterns are used in practice, a field that currently lacks empirical data.

</details>


### [19] [Towards Better Linux Kernel Fault Localization: Leveraging Contrastive Reasoning and Hierarchical Context Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00562)
*Haichi Wang,Ruiguo Yu,Yesong Pang,Yingquan Zhao,Junjie Chen,Jiajun Jiang,Zan Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CoHiKer是一种针对Linux内核的LLM-based故障定位技术，通过对比推理和层次化上下文分析，在文件级和方法级的Top-1定位准确率上分别提升高达26.07%和56.85%，同时降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Linux内核调试因代码库庞大、架构复杂和底层编程细节而极具挑战性。现有FL技术（包括传统方法和LLM方法）虽在通用软件中有效，但缺乏内核特定知识的深度整合，难以进行根因识别和细粒度定位。

Method: CoHiKer提出两项核心创新：(1)对比推理（contrastive reasoning）——通过分析精心突变的通过/失败测试用例之间的行为差异来识别根因；(2)层次化上下文分析（hierarchical context analysis）——整合崩溃报告、系统调用语义、文件间依赖和内核特定特征，系统性地将定位范围从文件缩小到方法。通过分解定位任务并结构化LLM提示，实现语义层面的推理。

Result: 在扩展的Linux内核bug数据集上，CoHiKer优于五种基线方法：文件级Top-1准确率提升26.07%，方法级提升56.85%，同时token消耗分别降低8.84%和28.9%。在非内核数据集上也表现出强泛化性，文件级和方法级Top-1分别提升15.5%和5.3%。

Conclusion: CoHiKer通过将内核特定知识深度融入LLM推理过程，有效解决了现有FL方法在内核环境中的局限性，在定位精度和效率上均取得显著提升，且具备良好的跨领域泛化能力。

Abstract: Debugging the Linux kernel remains a formidable challenge due to its vast codebase, complex architecture, and low-level programming intricacies. Effective fault localization (FL) is thus essential for efficient kernel debugging and maintenance. While existing FL techniques (both traditional and LLM-based) have shown promise in general-purpose software, they are ill-suited for the kernel context. In particular, recent LLM-based techniques often treat bug reports and source code as plain text, lacking deep integration of kernel-specific knowledge, which limits their ability to identify root causes and achieve fine-grained localization. We present CoHiKer, a novel LLM-based FL technique tailored to the Linux kernel. CoHiKer introduces two key innovations: (1) contrastive reasoning, which identifies root causes by analyzing the behavioral divergence between carefully mutated passing and failing test cases, and (2) hierarchical context analysis, which systematically narrows the localization scope from files to methods by integrating crash reports, syscall semantics, inter-file dependencies, and kernel-specific features. Unlike prior techniques that rely on static understanding and full-code input, CoHiKer decomposes the localization task and enables structured LLM prompting to reason semantically over meaningful contexts. We evaluate CoHiKer on an extended Linux kernel bug dataset against five state-of-the-art baselines. CoHiKer consistently outperforms all competitors, improving Top-1 localization accuracy by up to 26.07% at the file level and 56.85% at the method level over state-of-the-art LLM-based baselines, while achieving up to 8.84% and 28.9% reductions in token consumption, respectively. Furthermore, CoHiKer demonstrates strong generalizability on the non-kernel dataset, with comparable gains (15.5% and 5.3% in Top-1 at file and method levels).

</details>


### [20] [SchedCheck: Schedule-Robustness Analysis for Event-Driven Block Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00623)
*Yuan Si,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SchedCheck工具，用于检测Scratch这类基于块的编程语言中因调度依赖导致的程序不确定性（schedule-sensitive behaviors）。通过形式化调度空间、定义调度鲁棒性、并采用偏序探索与依赖模型，在真实学生项目中发现约17-21%的并发项目存在调度敏感问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Scratch等块编程语言中，多个脚本（如绿旗脚本、广播、克隆）以协作调度方式并发执行，但语言本身未规定执行顺序。编辑、保存或复用项目时，图层顺序的改变会导致相同代码产生不同可观察结果，带来不确定性，影响初学者对程序行为的理解与调试。

Method: 形式化Scratch虚拟机能实现的调度空间为初始可执行目标顺序的排列，定义基于固定视野和观察透镜格点的调度鲁棒性。采用偏序探索，每个依赖等价类只执行一个调度。对于小项目，独立预言机验证所有可达结果；对于大项目，使用依赖模型下的代表调度替代阶乘级数量。SchedCheck基于生产级Scratch VM实现。

Result: 在224个真实学生项目中，至少21%的并发项目在评分透镜下是调度敏感的；随机采样公共项目复现率为17.6%。发现两个真实复刻项目因调度敏感导致字母排列不同。在32个人工构建的规范定义故障（四类）和人工故障对中，工具检测并定位所有调度故障，逻辑故障对照组报告无问题。预言机发现依赖模型的四个不完备缺口并已修复。

Conclusion: Scratch等块编程语言中存在不可忽视的调度敏感行为，SchedCheck能有效检测并定位这些问题。该方法具有泛化性，已在第二个协作事件循环上不变地实例化，适用于更广泛的并发事件驱动语言。

Abstract: Block-based languages such as Scratch let beginners assemble interactive programs from sprites and scripts. These programs are concurrent in practice: green-flag scripts, broadcasts, and clones run as cooperatively scheduled threads over shared sprite and stage state, and their authors never write a thread. We show that such programs contain schedule-sensitive behaviors whose observable result depends on an execution order the language leaves open. Editing, saving, or remixing a project can produce a copy with the same blocks but a different layer order, changing the order the virtual machine starts scripts. We formalize the schedule space a Scratch virtual machine can realize as the permutations of the initial executable-target order, and define schedule-robustness against a lattice of observation lenses over a fixed horizon. A partial-order exploration runs one schedule per dependence-equivalence class, and on projects small enough to enumerate, an independent oracle confirms it recovers every realizable outcome. On larger projects, representatives stand in for the factorial under the validated dependence model. SchedCheck implements this on the production Scratch VM. Across 224 real student projects, at least 21% of the concurrent ones are schedule-sensitive at the grading lens, and a uniform random sample of public projects replicates the rate at 17.6%, with two real remixes of a deployed animation arranging its letters differently. On hand-built fault pairs and a generated benchmark of 32 spec-defined faults across four classes, the tool detects and localizes every schedule fault, with a logic-fault control reporting clean. The oracle exposed four unsoundness gaps in the dependence model, all repaired. The method is parametric in the execution model, instantiating unchanged on a second cooperative event loop.

</details>


### [21] [The Perception and Impact of Non-inclusive Language in Software Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00626)
*Ahmad J. Tayeb,Mohammad D. Alahmadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过对1,212份完整问卷的分析，调查了软件开发者对非包容性术语（如whitelist/blacklist、master/slave等）的认知及其对团队动态、生产力、归属感和幸福感的影响。结果显示，总体上受访者报告较低的负面工作影响，但女性和非二元性别受访者以及美国居民更倾向于认为这些术语具有非包容性，且非二元性别和女性受访者报告了更高的负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管软件行业越来越多地推动用更包容的替代词替换非包容性术语，但关于开发者如何看待这些术语以及其持续使用如何影响他们的职业体验和归属感的实证研究几乎空白。本文旨在填补这一知识缺口。

Method: 作者对开源贡献者进行了大规模问卷调查，共收到1,517份回复，其中1,212份完整问卷被纳入分析。研究考察了开发者对非包容性术语的感知及其对团队动态、生产力、归属感和幸福感的影响，并分析了不同人口统计学群体之间的差异。

Result: 总体上，受访者报告的非包容性术语负面工作影响较低；但女性和非二元性别受访者以及美国居民更倾向于认为这些术语具有非包容性。在认为术语非包容的群体中，非二元性别受访者报告的总负面影响高于男性，而女性受访者在归属感方面报告了更高的负面影响。

Conclusion: 非包容性术语对整体开发者的平均负面影响不大，但对特定人口群体（尤其是女性和非二元性别者）的归属感和整体体验有显著负面影响，表明术语替换倡议对创造包容性开发环境具有重要价值。

Abstract: Terminology such as "whitelist/blacklist," "master/slave," "man-hours," or "dummy value" has long been part of the technical vocabulary used in software artifacts, including source code, version histories, and documentation. In recent years, however, many of these expressions have been recognized as potentially non-inclusive and unwelcoming to groups historically underrepresented in software development, such as people of color, women, and individuals with disabilities. Consequently, a growing movement within the software industry has sought to replace these terms with more inclusive alternatives. Despite these initiatives, little is empirically known about how software developers perceive such terminology or how its continued use may influence their professional experiences and sense of belonging.
  This paper addresses the knowledge gap by examining how software developers perceive non-inclusive terminology in software and its perceived impact on team dynamics, productivity, belonging, and well-being. We surveyed open-source contributors and received 1,517 responses, of which 1,212 were complete and analyzed. On average, respondents reported low negative workplace impact overall; however, perceptions and impacts varied by demographic group. Women and non-binary participants, as well as respondents residing in the United States, were more likely to view the terms as non-inclusive. Among those who considered the terminology non-inclusive, non-binary participants reported higher overall negative impacts than male respondents, and female participants reported higher impact specifically on their sense of belonging.

</details>


### [22] [Checked Program Recovery from Execution Video: A Sound Oracle for Untrusted Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00635)
*Yuan Si,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段验证机制（静态检查器+渲染器），使基于不可信生成器（如神经模型）的程序恢复变得可信，并在Scratch程序恢复任务中实现了零错误接受，80%的已知词汇程序获得静态证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从行为观测恢复程序（例如从执行视频恢复Scratch代码）时，生成器（神经模型或搜索）提出的候选程序没有正确性保证，需要一种方法使这种恢复过程具备可信赖性。

Method: 提出两级验证接口：静态检查器通过偏序商证明镜头等价性并签发证书（绝不接受错误程序）；渲染器仅能反驳或见证有限一致性（不能认证）。Vid2Prog流水线从视频和已知资产清单读取精灵运动、可见性和时序，无源代码生成候选程序，并用闭环渲染和重新恢复进行真值验证。

Result: 在246个标记差异对（含对抗性并发陷阱）上零错误接受；已知词汇恢复逐帧匹配源码，80%获得静态证书；真实项目（大多超出词汇）恢复率14%，系统从未用错误答案虚增该比率；前沿视觉语言模型单次恢复为零，加上验证循环后仅恢复少数，而结构化流水线恢复全部。

Conclusion: 针对不可信生成器的程序恢复，通过精心设计的验证不对称性（静态证明绝不误纳+渲染仅能力证反例）可以实现可信恢复，验证机制是弥合不可信生成器与可靠恢复之间差距的关键。

Abstract: A growing class of tools recovers a program from observations of its behavior using an untrusted generator, a neural model or a search, that proposes candidates with no correctness guarantee. We study how to make such recovery trustworthy, in the concrete setting of recovering a runnable Scratch program from a recording of its execution. The recording shows what the program does but never its code; many programs produce the same video, so the source cannot be recovered, and the right target is a program that behaves the same as far as the camera can tell, made precise with a lens. The core is a two-tier validation oracle with a deliberate verdict asymmetry. A static checker proves lens-equivalence to a reference and issues a certificate that, granting the partial-order independence quotient adequate, never accepts a wrong program; a renderer can only refute or witness finite agreement, never certify. Around it, Vid2Prog reads each sprite's motion, visibility, and timing from the video and a known-asset manifest and synthesizes a candidate source-free; a closed loop renders and runs recovery again for ground truth. Under the exact lens the oracle makes no false accept on 246 labeled differing pairs, including an adversarial battery built to trap its concurrency quotient; on inputs outside the vocabulary and on real projects it abstains or refutes, accepting none we test. In-vocabulary recoveries reproduce their source frame for frame and 80% earn a static certificate, while whole real projects, mostly outside the vocabulary, recover at 14%, a vocabulary-bound rate the system never inflates with a wrong answer. A frontier vision-language model recovers none of the matched programs single-shot, which oracle-in-the-loop repair lifts only to a few while the structured pipeline recovers all, the gap a sound checker makes for an untrusted generator.

</details>


### [23] [LLVM-Bench: Benchmarking and Advancing Large Language Models for LLVM Compiler Issue Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00700)
*Zhao Tian,Yingquan Zhao,Chenyao Suo,Meng Wang,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出了LLVM-Bench（首个大规模LLVM问题解决基准，包含423个真实任务）和LLVM-Gym（自动化评估平台），并基于此对多种LLM、检索配置和智能体进行了全面研究。实验发现当前方法存在补丁无效和构建失败等问题，进而提出LLVM-Ens轻量级集成方法，通过融合多种技术生成的补丁并过滤无效候选，最高将问题解决率提升至21.99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLVM编译基础设施规模庞大、复杂度高，问题解决过程劳动密集且充满挑战。尽管大语言模型在问题解决上已取得显著成功，但其在复杂的系统级LLVM编译器上的有效性仍未被充分探索，存在研究空白。

Method: 构建了LLVM-Bench基准（423个真实LLVM问题）和LLVM-Gym评估平台（自动化复现、打补丁、编译和测试）；对4种代表性LLM、6种检索配置和3种智能体进行系统研究；提出LLVM-Ens集成方法，通过扩展补丁空间、过滤错误和冗余候选来选取最优解。

Result: 当前基于LLM的问题解决技术在LLVM-Bench上表现有限，主要失败模式为补丁无效和构建失败；不同LLM和智能体之间存在强互补性；LLVM-Ens集成方法实现了最高21.99%的问题解决率。

Conclusion: LLVM-Bench和LLVM-Gym为系统级编译器问题解决研究提供了标准化评估基础；当前LLM方法在该领域仍有显著局限；集成多种技术路径的LLVM-Ens是提升解决率的有效方向。

Abstract: LLVM is a widely used compiler infrastructure whose scale and complexity make issue resolution labor-intensive and challenging. Although large language models (LLMs) have recently achieved remarkable success in issue resolution, their effectiveness on complex system-level LLVM compiler remains largely unexplored. To address this gap, we introduce LLVM-Bench, the first large-scale benchmark for LLVM issue resolution, containing 423 real-world, validated tasks collected from the LLVM project. We further develop LLVM-Gym, a scalable evaluation platform that automates issue reproduction, patch application, compiler building, and test execution. Using LLVM-Bench and LLVM-Gym, we conduct a comprehensive study of four representative LLMs, six retrieval configurations, and three agents. Our results show that current LLM-based issue resolution techniques remain limited on LLVM-Bench, with patch invalidity and build failures as the dominant failure modes. We further reveal a strong complementarity among different LLMs and agents, motivating LLVM-Ens, a lightweight ensemble approach that expands the patch space through integrating the patches generated by diverse techniques, filters incorrect and redundant candidates, and identifies the most promising solution. Our results show that LLVM-Ens achieves a resolution rate of up to 21.99%, further improving LLVM issue resolution.

</details>


### [24] [ClarifyCodeBench: Evaluating LLMs on Clarifying Ambiguous Requirements for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00711)
*Zheng Fang,Dongming Jin,Yihong dong,Yongmin Li,Kechi Zhang,Zhi Jin,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出ClarifyCodeBench基准，用于评估LLM在需求模糊场景下的交互式澄清能力。实验发现代码生成能力强不等同于需求澄清能力强，推理提升对识别模糊性帮助有限，且多模糊性场景下性能急剧下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成基准假设输入需求完美指定，忽略了实际需求工程中需求模糊、不完整、不明确的问题，LLM主动澄清意图的能力未被充分探索。

Method: 基于真实编程任务构建ClarifyCodeBench基准，包含多种模糊类型、对应澄清问题及标准答案；提出Turn-discounted Key Question Rate和Optimal Round Adherence两个评估指标，系统评估6个SOTA LLM。

Result: 三个关键发现：1）能力解耦——代码生成强不等于需求澄清能力强；2）推理悖论——增加计算思维提升代码正确性但对识别模糊性增益有限；3）多模糊性天花板——随模糊密度增加，澄清性能急剧下降。

Conclusion: 未来AI4SE研究应从静态代码合成转向交互式需求获取，LLM的主动澄清能力是关键但当前严重不足。

Abstract: Large Language Models have emerged as programming assistants. However, the efficacy of code generation is constrained by the quality of input requirements, which are frequently ambiguous, incomplete, or underspecified. While LLMs excel at one-shot code synthesis, their ability to proactively clarify intent remains underexplored, as a critical trait for robust software engineering. Existing benchmarks largely overlook this interactive bottleneck, assuming perfectly specified prompts that do not reflect the iterative nature of requirement elicitation. To bridge this gap, we introduce ClarifyCodeBench, a novel interactive benchmark for evaluating LLMs' capability in resolving requirement ambiguity. Constructed from real-world programming tasks, ClarifyCodeBench features high-quality manual annotations, including N unique ambiguity types, associated clarification questions, and corresponding ground-truth answers. Furthermore, we formalize two rigorous metrics to assess the interaction quality: Turn-discounted Key Question Rate, which penalizes inefficient questioning, and Optimal Round Adherence, which measures the precision of the elicitation process. We conduct a systematic evaluation of six state-of-the-art LLMs using ClarifyCodeBench. Our empirical results yield three critical insights: 1) Capability Decoupling: Strong code generation performance does not inherently translate to effective requirement clarification; 2) The Reasoning Paradox: While increased computational thinking enhances code correctness, it yields marginal gains in identifying ambiguities; 3) The Multi-ambiguity Ceiling: LLMs' clarification performance degrades sharply as the density of ambiguities increases, revealing a significant bottleneck in handling complex, real-world specifications. Our work underscores the necessity for future AI4SE research to transition from static synthesis to interactive elicitation.

</details>


### [25] [Stochastic Connectivity as the Foundation of a Runtime Model for Microservice Availability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00740)
*Anatoly A. Krasnovsky,Anna Maslovskaya*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机连通性的微服务端点运行时可用性形式化模型，用于弹性分析。该模型可从分布式追踪和部署元数据重构，支持架构假设分析，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟评估，在合成实验中与理论解吻合，揭示了边缘瓶颈、相关性故障等边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的故障注入和混沌工程实验评估微服务可用性成本高、操作风险大、难以重复执行；而分布式追踪和部署元数据虽易获取，但仅描述服务交互关系，无法量化端点级别的可用性属性。因此需要一种低成本、可重复、形式化的可用性评估方法。

Method: 提出一个基于随机连通性的形式化运行时可用性模型，包含：类型化服务依赖图、副本映射、节点和边状态的概率测度、请求特定成功谓词。语义上区分服务副本的计算失败与逻辑依赖的通信失败，可从追踪和部署工件重构模型，支持参数化的架构假设分析，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟进行分析。

Result: 合成实验表明模型在封闭形式理论解范围内与采样误差一致，并揭示了由边缘瓶颈、相关性故障、缺失追踪和时间依赖性故障导致的边界条件。

Conclusion: 该模型为微服务端点可用性提供了一种形式化、可重构、可模拟的评估框架，可作为故障注入的补充或前置分析工具；复制无法补偿瓶颈依赖带来的可用性损失。

Abstract: Microservice availability is commonly assessed by fault injection and chaos experiments, but such experiments are costly, operationally risky, and difficult to repeat for every architectural change. Distributed tracing and deployment metadata provide cheaper evidence, yet they usually remain descriptive: they show which services interacted, not what endpoint-level availability property follows. This paper proposes a formal runtime availability model based on stochastic connectivity for resilience-oriented analysis of microservice endpoints. It treats endpoint availability under explicit fault scenarios as a measurable facet of microservice resilience, combining a typed service-dependency graph, a replication map, a probability measure over node and edge states, and request-specific success predicates. Its semantics separates computational failures of service replicas from communication failures of logical dependencies, showing that replication cannot compensate for bottleneck dependencies. The model can be reconstructed from traces and deployment artifacts, parameterized for architectural what-if analysis, and analyzed by Monte Carlo simulation before or alongside fault injection. We define the model, its trace-to-model construction, elementary semantic properties, and a synthetic adequacy study. The study matches closed-form oracle cases within sampling error and exposes boundaries caused by edge bottlenecks, correlated failures, missing traces, and time-dependent failures.

</details>


### [26] [Knowledge-Enhanced Agentic Vulnerability Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00820)
*Sicong Cao,Hao Ma,Le Yu,Kangyi Ding,Xiaolei Liu,Terry Yue Zhuo,Bo Wang,Xingwei Lin,Xiaobing Sun,Linzhang Wang,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出KeaRepair，一种基于智能体的自动化漏洞修复方法，通过多维度漏洞知识提取、工具增强的推理和检索增强的补丁生成，在55个C/C++漏洞上修复率达83.64%，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动漏洞修复方法难以可靠识别漏洞根因，且未能充分利用历史修复知识指导补丁生成，导致实际效果不佳。

Method: 1) 从历史漏洞-补丁对中提取多维度漏洞知识，构建专用检索知识库；2) 采用工具增强的智能体进行ReAct风格推理，收集已验证的程序事实用于漏洞诊断；3) 基于诊断结果进行知识级检索增强的补丁生成，通过编译、PoC重放和测试套件执行的闭环验证迭代优化补丁。

Result: 在55个可复现的C/C++漏洞上显著优于现有方法；配合Gemini-3.1-Pro时修复46个漏洞，修复率83.64%；修复了6个基线方法均无法修复的独特漏洞；具有强跨语言泛化能力。

Conclusion: KeaRepair通过将补丁生成建立在验证的程序事实和高级漏洞知识基础上，有效提升了自动化漏洞修复的可靠性和效果。

Abstract: Frontier foundation models have changed the math on vulnerability discovery, but the bigger challenge is how the remediation side keeps up. Despite recent progresses in Automated Vulnerability Repair (AVR), current solutions struggle to reliably identify the root causes of vulnerabilities, and insufficiently utilize the prior fix knowledge to guide the patch generation process, thus undermining their effectiveness in practice.
  To address this gap, we propose KeaRepair, a novel agentic AVR approach that grounds patch generation in verified program facts and high-level vulnerability knowledge. Specifically, KeaRepair first extracts multi-dimensional vulnerability knowledge from historical vulnerability-patch pairs from dual complementary views, and constructs dedicated retrieval knowledge bases. It then employs a tool-augmented agent that performs ReAct-style reasoning to collect verified program facts for vulnerability diagnosis. Finally, based on the diagnostic results, KeaRepair performs knowledge-level retrieval-augmented patch generation and iteratively refines patches through a closed-loop validation process involving compilation, PoC replay, and test-suite execution. Experimental results show that KeaRepair significantly outperforms existing AVR approaches on 55 reproducible C/C++ vulnerabilities. When paired with Gemini-3.1-Pro, KeaRepair successfully repairs 46 vulnerabilities, achieving a repair rate of 83.64%. Moreover, KeaRepair fixes six unique vulnerabilities that none of the baselines can address, and further demonstrates strong cross-language generalizability.

</details>


### [27] [Petrify: Petri-net Based Analysis of Concurrency Properties in Java Bytecode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00830)
*Akshatha Shenoy,Carlo A. Furia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Petrify是一种将Java字节码程序编码为Petri网进行自动形式化验证的新技术，能在不牺牲可扩展性的前提下实现高表达力，支持并发属性（如死锁）的精确分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化形式化验证技术在表达力/精确性与可扩展性/实用性之间存在显著的权衡取舍，缺乏能在两者间取得平衡的并发属性验证方法。

Method: 将Java字节码程序的语义编码为Petri网，然后利用LoLA等模型检测工具进行分析。通过定位到字节码层面实现语言无关性。

Result: Petrify的Petri网编码足够精确以刻画程序的并发行为，同时又足够简洁，分析开销对参数规模不敏感。原型工具jPetrify可分析任何版本Java及部分Kotlin程序。

Conclusion: Petrify实现了表达力与实用性的独特平衡，且该方法可扩展到死锁之外的其他并发分析场景。

Abstract: The landscape of automated formal verification is populated by techniques that make prominently different trade-offs: some focus on expressiveness and precision, supporting the verification of complex properties; others favor scalability and practicality, so that they are applicable to larger programs using different features. This paper presents Petrify, a novel automated verification technique for concurrency properties that achieves a distinctive trade-off. Petrify encodes the semantics of Java bytecode programs into Petri nets (PNs), which can be analyzed by state-of-the-art model checking tools such as LoLA. As our experiments demonstrate, Petrify's approach offers an interesting combination of expressiveness and practicality: PNs are a fairly precise encoding of the concurrent behavior of programs; at the same time, Petrify's PN encoding is succinct, so that its analysis remains quite insensitive to parameter size. Another practical benefit of targeting bytecode is that jPetrify, the prototype tool that implements the Petrify technique, is applicable to programs written in any version of Java and even a subset of Kotlin (another language that compiles to Java bytecode) while other similar tools are limited to older versions of Java. While this paper's experiments focus on analyzing fundamental properties like deadlock, Petrify's approach lends itself to be extended to other kinds of concurrency analysis, which we plan to tackle in future work.

</details>


### [28] [From Registry to Repository: How AI Agent Skills Are Written, Adapted, and Maintained](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00911)
*Haoyu Gao,Jai Lal Lulla,Hong Yi Lin,Sebastian Baltes,Christoph Treude,Mansooreh Zahedi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对AI编码智能体技能（Skill）进行了首次实证研究，分析了18,463个公开技能和23,199个个人技能，发现技能复用主要为一次性复制操作，53%的技能在采用后从未修改，定制化主要适配本地环境，而行为契约几乎保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码智能体越来越多地依赖技能作为结构化上下文包（即SKILL.md文件），公共注册中心已托管数万技能，但技能一直被视作智能体能力，而非作为被编写、复用、定制和维护的软件制品进行研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，对技能作为工程制品进行实证研究。

Method: 从skills.sh挖掘18,463个公开技能，从5,876个GitHub仓库挖掘23,199个个人技能，识别出3,709个复用链接；采用基于LLM的SWEBOK知识领域分类、180个技能的主题分析，以及444个修改的定性编码。

Result: 软件构建知识领域占主导地位，伴随长尾专业领域；识别出六种内容类别和六种修改主题；复用主要为一次性复制（53%从未修改），修改以新增为主，定制化适配本地环境，演化增加领域知识，而行为契约几乎不变。

Conclusion: 维护工作应聚焦于项目特定绑定，注册中心和工具应支持整合领域知识技能的重构，避免技能在隔离环境中重复编写。

Abstract: AI coding agents increasingly rely on skills: structured context bundles, typically a SKILL.md file with a YAML header and Markdown body, loaded on demand for domain knowledge, workflows, and scripts. Public registries such as skills.sh now host tens of thousands of skills, making them an emerging unit of reuse in agent-based software engineering. Yet skills have largely been viewed as agent capabilities rather than software artefacts whose content and evolution shape agent behaviour. We present the first empirical study of AI agent skills as engineered artefacts that are authored, reused, customised and maintained, across public registries and personal-use repositories. We mined 18,463 skills from skills.sh and 23,199 personal-use skills from 5,876 GitHub repositories, identifying 3,709 reuse links. LLM-based classification into SWEBOK knowledge areas (KAs) shows Software Construction dominates alongside a long tail of specialised areas. A thematic analysis of 180 skills identifies six content categories. Qualitative coding of 444 modifications reveals six themes, of which reworking operational specifications and adapting knowledge and resources are the primary target of change. Our findings show that reuse is largely a one-time copy operation: most reused skills remain near-verbatim, 53\% are never modified after adoption, and subsequent local maintenance is overwhelmingly additive. Customisation primarily adapts skills to local environments, whereas evolution accretes new inline domain knowledge. Across both, a stable behavioural contract -- how a skill interacts with users, monitors runtime state, and recovers from failures -- remains almost untouched. These results suggest maintenance effort should focus on project-specific bindings, and that registries and tool support should enable consolidating the domain knowledge skills re-author in isolation.

</details>


### [29] [Delta Debugging in the Absence of Test Oracles Through Metamorphic Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00929)
*Mingyue Jiang,Yongqiang Tian,Tsong Yueh Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出DDMT方法，通过引入蜕变测试技术重新设计独立于测试预言机的测试函数，使Delta调试能够应用于缺乏测试预言机的程序，实验证明该方法在保持或提升约简效果和查询效率的同时，显著扩展了Delta调试的适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Delta调试技术依赖测试预言机来判断约简后的输入是否保持特定属性，然而对于缺乏测试预言机的程序（oracle-deficient programs），无法直接确定输出正确性，这严重限制了Delta调试技术的应用范围。现有方法难以解决这一预言机问题。

Method: DDMT的核心思想是重新设计一个独立于预言机的测试函数，并将其融入Delta调试的约简流程中。具体采用蜕变测试（Metamorphic Testing）这一基于属性且不依赖测试预言机的方法，建立基于蜕变测试的测试函数，替代Delta调试原有的测试函数，从而在不需测试预言机的情况下完成属性保持性验证。

Result: 在66个测试对象上，分别在有无测试预言机的场景下使用不同Delta调试方法进行评估。结果表明DDMT能够增强Delta调试的适用性，同时通常能保持或提升约简效果和查询效率。在适当配置下，DDMT相比相关Delta调试方法也能实现性能提升。

Conclusion: DDMT通过将蜕变测试与Delta调试相结合，有效解决了传统Delta调试对测试预言机的依赖问题，显著扩展了其应用范围，特别是在缺乏测试预言机的场景下具有重要实用价值。

Abstract: Delta debugging provides an automatic way to minimize a program input while preserving a certain property. However, its effectiveness fundamentally relies on the availability of test oracles to determine whether a reduced input still preserves the specific property. Consequently, the oracle problem substantially limits the applicability of existing delta debugging techniques, particularly for oracle-deficient programs where output correctness cannot be directly determined. To address this problem, this paper proposes a novel approach, DDMT, to enhance the applicability of delta debugging, especially facilitating its application to oracle-deficient programs. Our key insight is to redesign an oracle-independent test function and incorporate it into the reduction procedure of delta debugging such that the property-preservation validation can be accomplished without requiring a test oracle. To this end, DDMT employs the technique of metamorphic testing, which is a property-based and oracle-independent testing method. It establishes a metamorphic testing-based test function, using it as a replacement for the original test function adopted by delta debugging. The experiments evaluate DDMT on 66 subjects across both oracle-available and oracle-deficient scenarios, with different delta debugging approaches. The results positively confirm that DDMT can enhance the applicability of delta debugging while often preserving or improving reduction effectiveness and query efficiency. Furthermore, compared to the relevant delta debugging approaches, DDMT is also able to achieve performance improvements with proper configurations.

</details>


### [30] [Leveraging LLM-Based Agentic Systems to Generate Quantum Applications for Test Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00939)
*Ming Tao,Yuechen Li,Tao Yue,Man Zhang,Aitor Arrieta Marcos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: QPipe是一种基于LLM的多智能体架构，能够自主将自然语言需求转化为可执行的量子应用工作流，在20个真实世界基准测试中实现100%代码编译成功率和96.7%执行成功率，且多数情况下优于遗传算法基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将自然语言任务级需求转化为可执行的量子应用仍需要大量量子和编程专业知识，缺乏自动化工具降低门槛。

Method: 提出QPipe，一个基于LLM的多智能体架构，包含需求解析、公式化、代码生成、审查、执行和验证等专门智能体，自主将NL需求转化为可追踪的量子应用工作流。

Result: 在20个NL需求上，代码编译成功率100%，应用执行与结果组合成功率96.7%，平均每个需求耗时260.1秒、消耗1.89M tokens；成功执行的量子应用在多数情况下优于离线遗传算法基线。

Conclusion: 智能体协作能够支持从真实世界基准测试中生成可执行的量子应用以解决测试优化问题，且优势依赖于保留代码生成技能、任务知识、审查反馈和多智能体分解。

Abstract: Quantum computing is increasingly explored for software engineering (SE) optimization, but translating natural-language (NL) task-level requirements into executable quantum applications still demands substantial quantum and programming expertise. We present QPipe, a large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent architecture that autonomously turns NL requirements into traceable quantum-application workflows through specialized agents for requirement parsing, formulation, code generation, review, execution, and verification. We evaluate QPipe on 20 NL requirements, each associated with a real-world benchmark and a test-optimization problem. QPipe successfully completes the key stages of quantum-application generation across requirements, achieving average rates of 100% for code compilation and 96.7% for application execution and final-result combination, with average generation costs of 260.1 seconds and 1.89M tokens per requirement. Among the generated quantum applications that execute successfully, the returned solutions outperform the offline genetic algorithm baseline in most cases. Ablation results further show that QPipe's advantage depends on retaining code-generation skills, task knowledge, review feedback, and multi-agent decomposition. These results indicate that agentic coordination can support generation of executable quantum applications for tackling test optimization problems from real-world benchmarks.

</details>


### [31] [SWE-Doctor: Guiding Software Engineering Agents with Runtime Diagnosis from Multi-Faceted Bug Reproduction Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00990)
*Yaoqi Guo,Yang Liu,Jie M. Zhang,Yun Ma,Yiling Lou,Zhenpeng Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出SWE-Doctor代理，通过多维度Bug复现测试的运行时诊断信息来指导补丁生成，在SWE-bench基准上平均解决率达75.7%（Verified）和59.4%（Pro），优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的软件工程代理虽使用Bug复现测试（BRT）进行补丁验证，但BRT能否辅助更核心的补丁生成阶段尚不明确。初步研究发现直接使用BRT引导补丁生成效果不佳：fail-to-fail类型的BRT会误导代理，而fail-to-pass类型的BRT带来的收益有限甚至为负。分析发现fail-to-pass BRT仅覆盖问题的一种表现导致补丁不完整，fail-to-fail BRT作为补丁生成目标不可靠。

Method: SWE-Doctor首先为问题报告中所述的不同行为需求生成多维度BRT，然后执行并调试这些BRT以构建运行时诊断记录，最后结合诊断信息与BRT生成过程中推断的定位信息来指导补丁生成，减少部分补丁问题。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified和SWE-bench Pro上使用5种LLM后端进行评估，SWE-Doctor在所有10个LLM-基准组合中均优于现有代理，平均解决率在Verified上达75.7%，在Pro上达59.4%。在更具挑战性的SWE-bench Pro上，相比基线代理平均提升8.0-8.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 多维度BRT的运行时诊断信息能有效提升补丁生成质量，克服了单一BRT覆盖不全和不可靠的问题。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based software engineering agents are increasingly developed to resolve software issues by generating patches from issue reports and code repositories. Bug reproduction tests (BRTs) are an important building block for such agents and have been shown useful for patch validation. However, it remains unclear whether BRTs can also help the more central stage of patch generation. We first conduct a preliminary study and find that directly using advanced BRT generators to guide patch generation is not beneficial: fail-to-fail BRTs can mislead agents, while even fail-to-pass BRTs bring limited or negative gains. Our analysis reveals two reasons: fail-to-pass BRTs may cover only one manifestation of the reported issue, leading to partial patches, whereas fail-to-fail BRTs are unreliable as direct patch-generation targets. Motivated by these insights, we propose SWE-Doctor, a software issue resolution agent that guides patch generation with runtime diagnoses derived from multi-faceted BRT executions. SWE-Doctor first generates multi-faceted BRTs for different behavioral requirements stated in the issue, then executes and debugs these BRTs to construct runtime-grounded diagnosis records, and finally uses the diagnoses together with localization information inferred during BRT generation to guide patch generation and reduce partial patches. We evaluate SWE-Doctor on Python bug-fixing issues from the widely adopted SWE-bench Verified and SWE-bench Pro across five LLM backends. SWE-Doctor consistently outperforms existing agents across all 10 LLM-benchmark combinations, achieving average resolution rates of 75.7% on SWE-bench Verified and 59.4% on SWE-bench Pro. In particular, on the more challenging SWE-bench Pro, SWE-Doctor improves the average resolution rate by 8.0-8.9 percentage points over the baseline agents.

</details>


### [32] [Identifying Effective Program Comprehension Strategies through Gaze Transitions over Syntactic Elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01042)
*Kyogo Horikawa,Hidetake Uwano,Haruhiko Yoshioka*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究将眼动追踪数据转换为语法节点间的转换，分析正确与错误完成程序理解任务的参与者在注视比例和视线转换模式上的差异，发现成功参与者表现出更系统化的语法元素间转换，暗示他们使用了结构化阅读策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有眼动追踪研究多基于屏幕坐标，虽已有将眼动数据映射到抽象语法树节点的方法，但任务正确性与特定语法元素眼动特征之间的关系仍不明确。

Method: 将眼动追踪数据转换为语法节点间的转换，分析注视比例和视线转换模式，并比较正确组与错误组之间的差异。

Result: 正确组与错误组在视线转换模式上存在显著差异，成功参与者展现出更系统化的语法元素间转换。

Conclusion: 成功参与者使用结构化阅读策略，这可能是影响程序理解任务正确性的关键因素。

Abstract: Program comprehension is a central research topic in software engineering, focusing on how developers understand a program's structure, behavior, and intent. Eye-tracking studies have traditionally relied on display-based measurements, where gaze positions are represented as screen coordinates. However, syntax-based analyses have recently emerged. Prior work proposed methods to convert eye movements into transitions between nodes in an abstract syntax tree, but the relationship between task correctness and eye-movement features for specific syntactic elements remains unclear. This study converts eye-tracking data into transitions between syntactic nodes and analyzes fixation proportions and gaze transition patterns. We investigate the relationship between these patterns and task correctness, comparing correct and incorrect groups. Our results reveal distinct differences in gaze transition patterns between the two groups. In particular, successful participants exhibit more systematic transitions across syntactic elements, suggesting the use of structured reading strategies.

</details>


### [33] [AutoRestTest at the SBFT 2026 Tool Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01063)
*Tyler Stennett,Myeongsoo Kim,Saurabh Sinha,Alessandro Orso*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AutoRestTest 是一种结合语义属性依赖图、多智能体强化学习和大型语言模型的REST API测试工具，在SBFT 2026 REST League的11个API上排名第一，平均每API检测到67.09个独特服务器错误并成功处理17.27个操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒REST API测试面临大输入空间和复杂操作间依赖关系的挑战，现有方法难以高效探索大型API输入空间。

Method: 提出AutoRestTest框架，结合语义属性依赖图（SPDG）、多智能体强化学习（MARL）和大型语言模型（LLM），智能地探索大规模API输入空间。

Result: 在SBFT 2026 REST League的11个API（317个操作，平均每API约29个操作）上，在故障检测、整体效率和整体有效性三个评估类别中均排名第一，一小时测试预算下平均每API检测到67.09个独特服务器错误并成功处理17.27个操作。

Conclusion: AutoRestTest通过融合语义依赖图、多智能体强化学习和LLM，有效提升了黑盒REST API测试的性能，并在基准评测中取得领先结果。

Abstract: Large input spaces and complex inter-operation dependencies make black-box REST API testing challenging. AutoRestTest combines a Semantic Property Dependency Graph, multi-agent reinforcement learning, and large language models to intelligently explore large API input spaces. In the SBFT 2026 REST League, AutoRestTest ranked first in all three evaluation categories -- fault detection, overall efficiency, and overall effectiveness -- on 11 APIs (317 operations, approximately 29 per API), averaging 67.09 unique server errors and 17.27 successfully processed operations per API under a one-hour testing budget.

</details>


### [34] [Cheap Code, Costly Judgment: A Case Study on Governable Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01087)
*James C. Davis,Paschal C. Amusuo,Tanmay Singla,Berk Çakar,Kirsten A. Davis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过一位专家工程师12周的第一人称案例研究，提出"治理转换"中程理论模型，解释如何通过工程判断将AI代理编程中的结构性失败转化为持久治理机制，从而实现对高速度AI驱动开发的可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI将软件工程从稀缺实现努力转向充裕、低成本代码生产，改变了核心工程问题：不再是AI能否生成有用代码，而是如何组织架构、工具、证据和反馈循环，使AI中介的开发保持可检查、可纠正和可维护。

Method: 第一人称案例研究：一位专家软件工程师在12周内使用前沿AI编码代理构建文档可访问性修复系统。实证记录包括88篇现场笔记、420 KLOC生产代码、1.16 MLOC测试/检查/文档/代理工具。

Result: 提出了"治理转换"中程理论，阐述为过程模型：代理实现速度暴露反复出现的结构性失败类别，工程判断通过将这些失败转换为持久治理机制来维持开发速度。与从已知义务推导控制的现有治理模型不同，治理转换解释控制是如何从代理工作中才可见的失败中被发现的。

Conclusion: 该模型可做出可测试预测，并对软件工程研究和实践有重要启示意义。

Abstract: Generative AI is shifting software engineering from a practice organized around scarce implementation effort toward one organized around abundant, low-cost code production. This shift changes the central engineering problem: not whether AI can generate useful code, but how engineers organize architectures, tools, evidence, and feedback loops so that AI-mediated development remains inspectable, correctable, and maintainable.
  We study this problem through a first-person case study: a 12-week development effort in which a single expert software engineer used frontier AI coding agents to build a document accessibility remediation system. The empirical record comprises 88 contemporaneous field notes, 420 KLOC of production code, and 1.16 MLOC of tests, lints, supporting documentation, and agent tooling.
